    proc remove(path: Str)

    proc is_dir(path: Str) returns Bool
    proc is_file(path: Str) returns Bool
    func is_absolute(path: Str) returns Bool
    proc remove_tree(path: Str)
    proc rename(src: Str, dst: Str)
    proc new(path: Str, is_write: Bool) returns File
    proc update(path: Str) returns File
    proc append(path: Str) returns File
    proc write(self: File, s: Str) returns ref File
    proc read_all(self: File) returns Str
    proc read(self: File, count: I64) returns Str
    proc tell(self: File) returns I64
    proc seek(self: File, pos: I64) returns ref File
    proc seek_cur(self: File, delta: I64) returns ref File
    proc seek_end(self: File, delta: I64) returns ref File
    func delete(own self: File, call_free: Bool = true)
    proc fwrite(self: File, parts: ..Str) returns ref File
    func size() returns U64
    func clone(self: File) returns File
}

struct Cmd {

namespace:
    proc new(args: ..Str) returns Cmd

    proc args(self: Cmd, more: ..Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc cwd(self: Cmd, dir: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc env(self: Cmd, name: Str, value: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc stdout_to(self: Cmd, path: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc stderr_to(self: Cmd, path: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc stderr_to_stdout(self: Cmd) returns ref Cmd
    proc capture(self: Cmd) returns ref Cmd
    proc capture_out(self: Cmd) returns ref Cmd
    proc render(self: Cmd) returns Str
    proc start(self: Cmd) returns I64
    proc wait(self: Cmd) returns I64
    proc poll(self: Cmd) returns Bool
    proc status(self: Cmd) returns I64
    proc run(self: Cmd) returns I64
    proc run_checked(self: Cmd)
    proc out(self: Cmd) returns Str
    proc err(self: Cmd) returns Str
    func delete(own self: Cmd, call_free: Bool = true)
    func size() returns U64
    func clone(self: Cmd) returns Cmd
}

    elem_delete : DeleteFn
namespace:
    func new_type_name(elem_type: Str) returns DynVec
    func new(T: Type) returns DynVec
    func with_capacity(T: Type, n: U64) returns DynVec
    func new_like(elem_size: U64, elem_kind: U64, elem_clone: CloneFn, elem_delete: DeleteFn, n: U64) returns DynVec
    func len(self: DynVec) returns U64
    func clear(mut self: DynVec)
    func delete_marked(mut self: DynVec, marks: Dynamic)
    func push(mut self: DynVec, own val: Dynamic)
    func append(mut self: DynVec, own other: DynVec)
    func move_from(mut self: DynVec, mut other: DynVec)
    func move_to(mut self: DynVec, mut dest: DynVec)
    func unsafe_get(self: DynVec, i: U64) returns ref Dynamic
    func get(self: DynVec, i: U64) returns ref Dynamic throws OutOfBounds
    func pop(mut self: DynVec) returns Dynamic
    func remove_at(mut self: DynVec, i: U64) throws OutOfBounds
    func truncate(mut self: DynVec, n: U64)
    func take_prefix(mut self: DynVec, n: U64) returns DynVec
    func take(mut self: DynVec) returns DynVec
    func unsafe_set(mut self: DynVec, i: U64, own val: Dynamic)
    func set(mut self: DynVec, i: U64, own val: Dynamic) throws OutOfBounds
    func push_take(mut self: DynVec, mut src: DynVec, i: U64)
    func delete(own self: DynVec, call_free: Bool = true)
    func clone(self: DynVec) returns DynVec
    func size() returns U64
}

}
